        Idea:
implement general agent-managed scheduled tasks end-to-end.

Context and requirements:
- Existing system has scheduled_jobs worker/handlers and run_agentic_job(user, trigger_metadata).
- Add a general scheduled job type for agent-created future briefs.
- Scheduled tasks can be one-shot or recurring.
- At due time, the scheduler should trigger the normal agent loop with kind=scheduled_task, no inbound message, and context containing task brief/job id/recurrence/delayed info.
- The agent can use all normal tools, send messages, create/cancel/update/list scheduled tasks, and self-cancel or mutate recurrence.
- Recurrence should be represented durably and reseed next occurrence after successful fire.
- It must integrate with existing pause/recovery/audit/tool-call patterns and tests.
- Keep edits scoped. Do not revert unrelated user changes.

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas/migrations/0017_scheduled_tasks.sql\n+BEGIN;\n+\n+ALTER TABLE scheduled_jobs DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS scheduled_jobs_job_type_check;\n+ALTER TABLE scheduled_jobs\n+    ADD CONSTRAINT scheduled_jobs_job_type_check\n+    CHECK (job_type IN (\n+        'checkin',\n+        'weekly_summary',\n+        'oob_review',\n+        'watch_item_due',\n+        'heartbeat',\n+        'deferred_turn',\n+        'scheduled_task'\n+    ));\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_scheduled_jobs_pending_scheduled_task_lookup\n+    ON scheduled_jobs (user_id, scheduled_for)\n+    WHERE status = 'pending' AND job_type = 'scheduled_task';\n+\n+CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_scheduled_jobs_pending_scheduled_task_source_job\n+    ON scheduled_jobs ((context->>'source_job_id'))\n+    WHERE status = 'pending'\n+      AND job_type = 'scheduled_task'\n+      AND context->>'source_job_id' IS NOT NULL;\n+\n+COMMIT;\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
implement general agent-managed scheduled tasks end-to-end.

Context and requirements:
- Existing system has scheduled_jobs worker/handlers and run_agentic_job(user, trigger_metadata).
- Add a general scheduled job type for agent-created future briefs.
- Scheduled tasks can be one-shot or recurring.
- At due time, the scheduler should trigger the normal agent loop with kind=scheduled_task, no inbound message, and context containing task brief/job id/recurrence/delayed info.
- The agent can use all normal tools, send messages, create/cancel/update/list scheduled tasks, and self-cancel or mutate recurrence.
- Recurrence should be represented durably and reseed next occurrence after successful fire.
- It must integrate with existing pause/recovery/audit/tool-call patterns and tests.
- Keep edits scoped. Do not revert unrelated user changes.

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas/app/services/scheduled_task_recurrence.py\n+\"\"\"Recurrence helpers for agent-managed scheduled tasks.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from collections.abc import Mapping\n+from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta\n+from typing import Any\n+\n+\n+class RecurrenceError(ValueError):\n+    \"\"\"Raised when a scheduled-task recurrence rule is malformed.\"\"\"\n+\n+\n+def _aware_datetime(value: Any, field: str) -> datetime:\n+    if isinstance(value, datetime):\n+        parsed = value\n+    elif isinstance(value, str):\n+        try:\n+            parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace(\"Z\", \"+00:00\"))\n+        except ValueError as exc:\n+            raise RecurrenceError(f\"{field} must be an ISO datetime\") from exc\n+    else:\n+        raise RecurrenceError(f\"{field} must be an aware datetime\")\n+    if parsed.tzinfo is None or parsed.utcoffset() is None:\n+        raise RecurrenceError(f\"{field} must include a timezone\")\n+    return parsed.astimezone(UTC)\n+\n+\n+def _positive_int(value: Any, field: str) -> int:\n+    try:\n+        parsed = int(value)\n+    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:\n+        raise RecurrenceError(f\"{field} must be an integer\") from exc\n+    if parsed < 1:\n+        raise RecurrenceError(f\"{field} must be at least 1\")\n+    return parsed\n+\n+\n+def _nonnegative_int(value: Any, field: str) -> int:\n+    try:\n+        parsed = int(value)\n+    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:\n+        raise RecurrenceError(f\"{field} must be an integer\") from exc\n+    if parsed < 0:\n+        raise RecurrenceError(f\"{field} must be at least 0\")\n+    return parsed\n+\n+\n+def normalize_recurrence(recurrence: Mapping[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n+    \"\"\"Return a canonical v1 recurrence rule, or None for one-shot schedules.\"\"\"\n+\n+    if recurrence is None:\n+        return None\n+    if not isinstance(recurrence, Mapping):\n+        raise RecurrenceError(\"recurrence must be an object\")\n+\n+    kind = str(recurrence.get(\"type\") or recurrence.get(\"frequency\") or \"one_shot\").strip().lower()\n+    if kind in {\"\", \"none\", \"null\", \"one_shot\", \"once\"}:\n+        return None\n+    if kind not in {\"daily\", \"weekly\"}:\n+        raise RecurrenceError(\"recurrence.type must be daily or weekly\")\n+\n+    normalized: dict[str, Any] = {\n+        \"version\": 1,\n+        \"type\": kind,\n+        \"interval\": _positive_int(recurrence.get(\"interval\", 1), \"recurrence.interval\"),\n+    }\n+    if recurrence.get(\"cancelled\") or recurrence.get(\"canceled\"):\n+        normalized[\"cancelled\"] = True\n+    if \"until\" in recurrence and recurrence[\"until\"] is not None:\n+        normalized[\"until\"] = _aware_datetime(recurrence[\"until\"], \"recurrence.until\").isoformat()\n+    if \"remaining_occurrences\" in recurrence and recurrence[\"remaining_occurrences\"] is not None:\n+        normalized[\"remaining_occurrences\"] = _nonnegative_int(\n+            recurrence[\"remaining_occurrences\"],\n+            \"recurrence.remaining_occurrences\",\n+        )\n+\n+    if kind == \"weekly\":\n+        weekdays_value = recurrence.get(\"weekdays\")\n+        if not isinstance(weekdays_value, list) or not weekdays_value:\n+            raise RecurrenceError(\"recurrence.weekdays must be a non-empty list for weekly rules\")\n+        weekdays = sorted({_nonnegative_int(day, \"recurrence.weekdays\") for day in weekdays_value})\n+        if any(day > 6 for day in weekdays):\n+            raise RecurrenceError(\"recurrence.weekdays values must be between 0 and 6\")\n+        normalized[\"weekdays\"] = weekdays\n+\n+    return normalized\n+\n+\n+def next_occurrence_utc(current_occurrence: datetime, recurrence: Mapping[str, Any] | None) -> datetime | None:\n+    \"\"\"Calculate the next UTC occurrence after the current due time.\"\"\"\n+\n+    normalized = normalize_recurrence(recurrence)\n+    if normalized is None or normalized.get(\"cancelled\"):\n+        return None\n+    if normalized.get(\"remaining_occurrences\") is not None and normalized[\"remaining_occurrences\"] <= 1:\n+        return None\n+\n+    current = _aware_datetime(current_occurrence, \"current_occurrence\")\n+    interval = normalized[\"interval\"]\n+    if normalized[\"type\"] == \"daily\":\n+        candidate = current + timedelta(days=interval)\n+    else:\n+        candidate = _next_weekly_occurrence(current, normalized[\"weekdays\"], interval)\n+\n+    until = normalized.get(\"until\")\n+    if until is not None and candidate > _aware_datetime(until, \"recurrence.until\"):\n+        return None\n+    return candidate\n+\n+\n+def recurrence_after_fire(recurrence: Mapping[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n+    \"\"\"Return the recurrence payload to persist for the next occurrence.\"\"\"\n+\n+    normalized = normalize_recurrence(recurrence)\n+    if normalized is None or normalized.get(\"cancelled\"):\n+        return None\n+    remaining = normalized.get(\"remaining_occurrences\")\n+    if remaining is None:\n+        return normalized\n+    if remaining <= 1:\n+        return None\n+    updated = dict(normalized)\n+    updated[\"remaining_occurrences\"] = remaining - 1\n+    return updated\n+\n+\n+def _next_weekly_occurrence(current: datetime, weekdays: list[int], interval: int) -> datetime:\n+    week_start = current - timedelta(days=current.weekday())\n+    for day_offset in range(1, (interval + 1) * 7 + 1):\n+        candidate = current + timedelta(days=day_offset)\n+        week_delta = (candidate.date() - week_start.date()).days // 7\n+        if candidate.weekday() in weekdays and week_delta % interval == 0:\n+            return candidate\n+    raise RecurrenceError(\"could not calculate next weekly occurrence\")\n*** End Patch\n"
}

implement general agent-managed scheduled tasks end-to-end.

Context and requirements:
- Existing system has scheduled_jobs worker/handlers and run_agentic_job(user, trigger_metadata).
- Add a general scheduled job type for agent-created future briefs.
- Scheduled tasks can be one-shot or recurring.
- At due time, the scheduler should trigger the normal agent loop with kind=scheduled_task, no inbound message, and context containing task brief/job id/recurrence/delayed info.
- The agent can use all normal tools, send messages, create/cancel/update/list scheduled tasks, and self-cancel or mutate recurrence.
- Recurrence should be represented durably and reseed next occurrence after successful fire.
- It must integrate with existing pause/recovery/audit/tool-call patterns and tests.
- Keep edits scoped. Do not revert unrelated user changes.

Integers use Python weekday numbering: Monday=0 through Sunday=6.\",\n+    )\n+    until: datetime | None = Field(\n+        default=None,\n+        description=\"Optional inclusive UTC end bound. Required for one-shot tasks. > DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas

        Idea:
implement general agent-managed scheduled tasks end-to-end.

Context and requirements:
- Existing system has scheduled_jobs worker/handlers and run_agentic_job(user, trigger_metadata).
- Add a general scheduled job type for agent-created future briefs.
- Scheduled tasks can be one-shot or recurring.
- At due time, the scheduler should trigger the normal agent loop with kind=scheduled_task, no inbound message, and context containing task brief/job id/recurrence/delayed info.
- The agent can use all normal tools, send messages, create/cancel/update/list scheduled tasks, and self-cancel or mutate recurrence.
- Recurrence should be represented durably and reseed next occurrence after successful fire.
- It must integrate with existing pause/recovery/audit/tool-call patterns and tests.
- Keep edits scoped. Do not revert unrelated user changes.

        Batch framing:
        - Execute batch 4 of 9.

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas

        Idea:
implement general agent-managed scheduled tasks end-to-end.

Context and requirements:
- Existing system has scheduled_jobs worker/handlers and run_agentic_job(user, trigger_metadata).
- Add a general scheduled job type for agent-created future briefs.
- Scheduled tasks can be one-shot or recurring.
- At due time, the scheduler should trigger the normal agent loop with kind=scheduled_task, no inbound message, and context containing task brief/job id/recurrence/delayed info.
- The agent can use all normal tools, send messages, create/cancel/update/list scheduled tasks, and self-cancel or mutate recurrence.
- Recurrence should be represented durably and reseed next occurrence after successful fire.
- It must integrate with existing pause/recovery/audit/tool-call patterns and tests.
- Keep edits scoped. Do not revert unrelated user changes.

        Batch framing:
        - Execute batch 5 of 9.
        - Actionable task IDs for this batch: ['T6', 'T7']

        Idea:
implement general agent-managed scheduled tasks end-to-end.

Context and requirements:
- Existing system has scheduled_jobs worker/handlers and run_agentic_job(user, trigger_metadata).
- Add a general scheduled job type for agent-created future briefs.
- Scheduled tasks can be one-shot or recurring.
- At due time, the scheduler should trigger the normal agent loop with kind=scheduled_task, no inbound message, and context containing task brief/job id/recurrence/delayed info.
- The agent can use all normal tools, send messages, create/cancel/update/list scheduled tasks, and self-cancel or mutate recurrence.
- Recurrence should be represented durably and reseed next occurrence after successful fire.
- It must integrate with existing pause/recovery/audit/tool-call patterns and tests.
- Keep edits scoped. Do not revert unrelated user changes.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas

        Idea:
implement general agent-managed scheduled tasks end-to-end.

Context and requirements:
- Existing system has scheduled_jobs worker/handlers and run_agentic_job(user, trigger_metadata).
- Add a general scheduled job type for agent-created future briefs.
- Scheduled tasks can be one-shot or recurring.
- At due time, the scheduler should trigger the normal agent loop with kind=scheduled_task, no inbound message, and context containing task brief/job id/recurrence/delayed info.
- The agent can use all normal tools, send messages, create/cancel/update/list scheduled tasks, and self-cancel or mutate recurrence.
- Recurrence should be represented durably and reseed next occurrence after successful fire.
- It must integrate with existing pause/recovery/audit/tool-call patterns and tests.
- Keep edits scoped. Do not revert unrelated user changes.

scheduler_poll_interval_s=1)\n+    worker = ScheduledJobWorker(\n+        fake_pool,\n+        handlers=ScheduledJobHandlers(fake_pool).as_dict(),\n+        settings=settings,\n+        worker_id=\"scheduled-task-worker\",\n+    )\n+\n+    result = await worker.run_due_once(now=now)\n+\n+    assert result.retried == 1\n+    assert fake_pool.scheduled_jobs[job_id][\"status\"] == \"pending\"\n+    assert fake_pool.scheduled_jobs[job_id][\"attempt_count\"] == 1\n+    assert not [\n+        job\n+        for job in fake_pool.scheduled_jobs.values()\n+        if job[\"job_type\"] == \"scheduled_task\" and job.get(\"context\", {}).get(\"source_job_id\") == str(job_id)\n+    ]\n+\n+\n async def test_pause_claims_only_heartbeat_and_preserves_user_jobs(fake_pool):\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
implement general agent-managed scheduled tasks end-to-end.

Context and requirements:
- Existing system has scheduled_jobs worker/handlers and run_agentic_job(user, trigger_metadata).
- Add a general scheduled job type for agent-created future briefs.
- Scheduled tasks can be one-shot or recurring.
- At due time, the scheduler should trigger the normal agent loop with kind=scheduled_task, no inbound message, and context containing task brief/job id/recurrence/delayed info.
- The agent can use all normal tools, send messages, create/cancel/update/list scheduled tasks, and self-cancel or mutate recurrence.
- Recurrence should be represented durably and reseed next occurrence after successful fire.
- It must integrate with existing pause/recovery/audit/tool-call patterns and tests.
- Keep edits scoped. Do not revert unrelated user changes.

 \"failure_reason\": None,\n+        \"reasoning\": \"\",\n+    }\n+    ctx = TurnContext(turn_id, pool, user, partner, [], phase=\"write\")\n+\n+    created = await write_tools.schedule_task(\n+        ctx,\n+        ScheduleTaskInput(\n+            brief=\"Smoke recurring task.\",\n+            when=datetime(2026, 5, 5, 9, 30, tzinfo=UTC),\n+            recurrence={\"type\": \"daily\",         Idea:
implement general agent-managed scheduled tasks end-to-end.

Context and requirements:
- Existing system has scheduled_jobs worker/handlers and run_agentic_job(user, trigger_metadata).
- Add a general scheduled job type for agent-created future briefs.
- Scheduled tasks can be one-shot or recurring.
- At due time, the scheduler should trigger the normal agent loop with kind=scheduled_task, no inbound message, and context containing task brief/job id/recurrence/delayed info.
- The agent can use all normal tools, send messages, create/cancel/update/list scheduled tasks, and self-cancel or mutate recurrence.
- Recurrence should be represented durably and reseed next occurrence after successful fire.
- It must integrate with existing pause/recovery/audit/tool-call patterns and tests.
- Keep edits scoped. Do not revert unrelated user changes.
